```json
    "curve": [
      {"record_date": "2025-03-12", "se_od": -1.75, "se_os": -1.50},
      {"record_date": "2025-12-05", "se_od": -2.00, "se_os": -1.75},
      {"record_date": "2026-10-14", "se_od": -2.63, "se_os": -2.38}
    ],
    "suggestions": [
      "建议每 3 个月复查一次，重点监测眼轴长度",
      "增加户外活动时间 ≥2 小时/日",
      "保持每日戴镜 10 小时以上，注意用眼姿势"
    ],
    "disclaimer": "本报告为健康管理参考，不替代临床诊断，请结合医生面诊意见",
    "watermark": {"patient_name": "张××", "archive_no": "VA00001234",
                  "generated_at": "2026-11-02T11:50:00+08:00", "generated_by": "王护士"},
    "page_count": 2
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) `report_type ∈ {GROWTH_REPORT, PROGRESSION_SUMMARY}`；2) `GROWTH_REPORT` 要求有效记录点 ≥2，否则 `"1009033"`；3) 按 `months` 截取区间（默认 24 个月）；4) 组装患者/周期/视力总览/复查依从性/曲线/建议；5) **建议文案按 `risk_level` 从模板生成，医生可在打印前覆写**；6) 水印必须含生成人、档案号、时间；7) 页脚强制免责声明；8) **不返回任何金额**；9) 写审计（每次生成一份记录）。**错误码：** `"1009026"`、`"1009033"`、`"40001"`、`"109001"`。

#### API-34 批量报告生成任务

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/vision-archives/report/batch
```

```json
{
  "report_type": "PROGRESSION_SUMMARY",
  "template": "GENERAL",
  "archive_nos": ["VA00001234", "VA00001290", "VA00001301"],
  "filter": null,
  "copies": 1,
  "print": false
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "task_id": "RPT2026110200003",
    "report_type": "PROGRESSION_SUMMARY",
    "total": 3,
    "accepted": 3,
    "rejected": [
      {"archive_no": "VA00001400", "reason": "RECORD_NOT_ENOUGH"}
    ],
    "status": "PROCESSING",
    "estimated_seconds": 6,
    "created_at": "2026-11-02T11:55:00+08:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) `archive_nos` 与 `filter` 二选一，单次 ≤200 份 `"1009034"`；2) 逐个校验可达性（记录点数量），不合格的进 `rejected` 并说明原因；3) 生成异步任务（Celery `m10.followup.report_batch`），返回 `task_id`；4) 完成后写打印历史与审计；5) `print = true` 时同时下发打印队列。**错误码：** `"1009033"`、`"1009034"`、`"40001"`、`"109001"`。

#### API-35 依从性统计

**请求**

```
GET /api/v1/m10/followups/statistics?period=MONTH&period_from=2026-10&period_to=2026-10&store_id=0&followup_type=POST_ORTHOK
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "period": {"stat_period": "MONTH", "period_from": "2026-10", "period_to": "2026-10"},
    "core": {"task_total": 1584, "task_completed": 1348, "task_on_time": 1211,
             "task_missed": 236, "on_time_rate": 76.45, "completion_rate": 85.10,
             "overdue_rate": 14.90, "recall_rate": 32.18, "avg_delay_days": 2.1},
    "trend": [
      {"stat_date": "2026-08-01", "on_time_rate": 74.20, "overdue_rate": 16.80},
      {"stat_date": "2026-09-01", "on_time_rate": 75.61, "overdue_rate": 15.42},
      {"stat_date": "2026-10-01", "on_time_rate": 76.45, "overdue_rate": 14.90}
    ],
    "by_rule": [
      {"rule_code": "FR001", "rule_name": "普通框架配镜后复查", "node_family": "D14",
       "task_total": 186, "on_time_rate": 81.72, "overdue_rate": 9.14,
       "dropout_rate": null, "avg_delay_days": 1.2, "target_met": true},
      {"rule_code": "FR006", "rule_name": "儿童近视监测", "node_family": "M3",
       "task_total": 512, "on_time_rate": 65.43, "overdue_rate": 24.61,
       "dropout_rate": null, "avg_delay_days": 4.6, "target_met": false}
    ],
    "orthok_dropout": {
      "orthok_plan_cnt": 412, "dropout_plan_cnt": 9, "dropout_rate": 2.18,
      "max_overdue_days": 68, "blocked_renew_cnt": 9,
      "reason_breakdown": [
        {"reason": "CONTACT_FAILED", "cnt": 4},
        {"reason": "PATIENT_REFUSED", "cnt": 2},
        {"reason": "TRANSFERRED", "cnt": 1},
        {"reason": "FINANCE", "cnt": 2}
      ]
    },
    "by_owner": [
      {"owner_id": 2001, "owner_name": "李护士", "store_id": 101, "task_total": 198,
       "on_time_rate": 82.31, "overdue_rate": 8.19, "recall_rate": 41.22}
    ],
    "is_final": true,
    "calc_version": "V1.0",
    "calc_at": "2026-11-08T01:50:00+08:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 从 `m10_followup_stat` 聚合（预聚合表，不实时扫业务表）；2) 比率分母为 0 返回 `null`（前端显示 `—`）；3) `dropout_rate` 只在 `followup_type = POST_ORTHOK` 上有值；4) `is_final` 取自 `D+7` 定稿标记，未定稿时返回 `false` 并允许前端提示；5) 达标判定：`on_time_rate ≥ 80%` 为 `target_met = true`；6) 结果缓存 300 秒。**错误码：** `"40001"`、`"1009037"`。

#### API-36 重算统计

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/followups/statistics/rebuild
```

```json
{
  "stat_period": "DAY",
  "date_from": "2026-10-01",
  "date_to": "2026-10-31",
  "store_id": 101,
  "reason": "10 月改期数据回补后重算"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "task_id": "STB2026110200001",
    "stat_period": "DAY",
    "date_range": {"from": "2026-10-01", "to": "2026-10-31"},
    "affected_rows": 310,
    "status": "PROCESSING",
    "note": "重算结果覆盖原记录并保留审计轨迹，已定稿日的数据重算需主管确认"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 权限 `m10:followup:config`；2) 区间跨度 ≤ `M10.FU.STAT_RECALC_DAYS`×4 或 366 天 `"1009037"`；3) 已定稿（`is_final = TRUE`）的日期重算需二次确认并写审计（口径变更必须留痕）；4) 异步重算，`ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE` 覆盖；5) 清统计缓存。**错误码：** `"1009037"`、`"40001"`、`"109001"`。

---
### 6.4 FastAPI 路由实现示例

```python
# app/api/v1/m10/followup.py
"""M10-09 复查与视力档案 —— 对外路由。

路由声明顺序要点：
  tasks/blocking/{patient_id} 必须声明在 tasks/{task_no} 之前，
  否则 "blocking" 会被当作 task_no 匹配掉。
  同时对 task_no / plan_no 加路径正则约束做双保险。
"""

import re
from typing import List, Optional

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Query, Request
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.database import get_db
from app.core.response import PageResult, success_response
from app.core.security import get_current_user, require_permission
from app.schemas.m10.followup import (
    FollowupPlanCreateIn, FollowupPlanQuery, FollowupRuleCreateIn,
    FollowupRulePreviewIn, FollowupTaskQuery, NotifySendIn,
    ProgressionCalcIn, ProgressionHandleIn, VisitArriveIn, VisitSubmitIn,
)
from app.services.m10.followup_plan_service import FollowupPlanService
from app.services.m10.followup_task_service import FollowupTaskService
from app.services.m10.followup_notify_service import FollowupNotifyService
from app.services.m10.followup_visit_service import FollowupVisitService
from app.services.m10.vision_archive_service import VisionArchiveService
from app.services.m10.progression_service import ProgressionService

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/v1/m10/followups", tags=["M10-09-复查"])

TASK_NO_PATTERN = r"^FT[0-9]{14}$"
PLAN_NO_PATTERN = r"^FC[0-9]{12}$"


# ==================== 规则 ====================

@router.get("/rules", summary="API-01 复查规则列表")
async def list_rules(
    trigger_type: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    status: Optional[str] = Query("ENABLED"),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:followup:view")),
):
    """按触发类型筛选规则；结果缓存 600 秒。"""
    svc = FollowupPlanService(db)
    items, total = await svc.list_rules(trigger_type=trigger_type, status=status,
                                        page=page, page_size=page_size)
    return success_response(PageResult(
        items=items, total=total, page=page, page_size=page_size,
        pages=(total + page_size - 1) // page_size,
    ))


@router.post("/rules/preview", summary="API-05 节点展开预览")
async def preview_rule(
    payload: FollowupRulePreviewIn,
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:followup:view")),
):
    """不落库，仅按规则与起始日展开节点。"""
    svc = FollowupPlanService(db)
    return success_response(await svc.preview(payload))


@router.post("/rules", summary="API-03 新建复查规则")
async def create_rule(
    payload: FollowupRuleCreateIn,
    request: Request,
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:followup:config")),
):
    """新建规则；幂等键 = rule_code。"""
    idem_key = request.headers.get("Idempotency-Key")
    svc = FollowupPlanService(db)
    return success_response(await svc.create_rule(payload, idem_key=idem_key, operator=user))


# ==================== 计划 ====================

@router.get("/plans", summary="API-07 复查计划分页列表")
async def list_plans(
    keyword: Optional[str] = Query(None, max_length=64),
    followup_type: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    status: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    rule_code: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    store_id: Optional[int] = Query(None, ge=0),
    overdue_only: bool = Query(False),
    dropout_only: bool = Query(False),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=200),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:followup:view")),
):
    """多条件筛选复查计划，按下次到期日升序。"""
    svc = FollowupPlanService(db)
    q = FollowupPlanQuery(
        keyword=keyword, followup_type=followup_type, status=status,
        rule_code=rule_code, store_id=store_id, overdue_only=overdue_only,
        dropout_only=dropout_only, page=page, page_size=page_size,
    )
    items, total = await svc.list_plans(q, user=user)
    return success_response(PageResult(
        items=items, total=total, page=page, page_size=page_size,
        pages=(total + page_size - 1) // page_size,
    ))


@router.post("/plans", summary="API-06 创建复查计划")
async def create_plan(
    payload: FollowupPlanCreateIn,
    request: Request,
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:followup:create")),
):
    """创建计划并展开全部节点任务（同一来源同规则幂等）。"""
    idem_key = request.headers.get("Idempotency-Key")
    svc = FollowupPlanService(db)
    return success_response(
        await svc.create_plan(payload, idem_key=idem_key, operator=user))


@router.get("/plans/{plan_no}", summary="API-08 复查计划详情")
async def get_plan(
    plan_no: str,
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:followup:view")),
):
    """含节点时间轴与进度。"""
    if not re.fullmatch(PLAN_NO_PATTERN, plan_no):
        raise _not_found("1009007", plan_no)
    svc = FollowupPlanService(db)
    return success_response(await svc.get_plan(plan_no, user=user))


@router.post("/plans/{plan_no}/terminate", summary="API-10 终止计划")
async def terminate_plan(
    plan_no: str,
    payload: dict,
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:followup:create")),
):
    """终止计划，未终结任务全部置 CANCELLED。"""
    svc = FollowupPlanService(db)
    return success_response(await svc.terminate(plan_no, payload, operator=user))


# ==================== 任务（blocking 必须在前）====================

@router.get("/tasks/blocking/{patient_id}", summary="API-14 阻断换片脱管查询")
async def blocking_tasks(
    patient_id: int,
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:followup:view")),
):
    """供 M10-04 换片入口校验：存在脱管则返回 blocked=true。"""
    svc = FollowupTaskService(db)
    return success_response(await svc.find_blocking_tasks(patient_id))


@router.get("/tasks", summary="API-12 复查任务分页列表")
async def list_tasks(
    status: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    followup_type: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    overdue_only: bool = Query(False),
    due_date_from: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    due_date_to: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=200),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:followup:view")),
):
    """支持状态/类型/到期区间/仅逾期筛选。"""
    svc = FollowupTaskService(db)
    q = FollowupTaskQuery(
        status=status, followup_type=followup_type, overdue_only=overdue_only,
        due_date_from=due_date_from, due_date_to=due_date_to,
        page=page, page_size=page_size,
    )
    items, total = await svc.list_tasks(q, user=user)
    return success_response(PageResult(
        items=items, total=total, page=page, page_size=page_size,
        pages=(total + page_size - 1) // page_size,
    ))


@router.get("/tasks/{task_no}", summary="API-13 复查任务详情")
async def get_task(
    task_no: str,
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:followup:view")),
):
    """含应做项目、窗口、上次到访摘要。"""
    if not re.fullmatch(TASK_NO_PATTERN, task_no):
        raise _not_found("1009013", task_no)
    svc = FollowupTaskService(db)
    return success_response(await svc.get_task(task_no))


@router.post("/tasks/{task_no}/arrive", summary="API-22 到访登记")
async def arrive_task(
    task_no: str,
    payload: VisitArriveIn,
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:followup:visit")),
):
    """任务转 ARRIVED 并生成到访号；一人一次，幂等。"""
    svc = FollowupVisitService(db)
    return success_response(await svc.arrive(task_no, payload, operator=user))


# ==================== 提醒召回 ====================

@router.get("/notify/queue", summary="API-17 今日触达队列")
async def notify_queue(
    notify_date: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    store_id: Optional[int] = Query(None, ge=0),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(50, ge=1, le=200),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:followup:call")),
):
    """含合规闸门预判，用于界面直接说明为何不能发。"""
    svc = FollowupNotifyService(db)
    return success_response(
        await svc.get_queue(notify_date=notify_date, store_id=store_id,
                            page=page, page_size=page_size, user=user))


@router.post("/notify/send", summary="API-18 发送提醒/召回")
async def send_notify(
    payload: NotifySendIn,
    request: Request,
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:followup:call")),
):
    """逐条过四条合规闸门（拒收静默/监护人/冷却/月上限）后发送。"""
    idem_key = request.headers.get("Idempotency-Key")
    svc = FollowupNotifyService(db)
    return success_response(
        await svc.send(payload, idem_key=idem_key, operator=user))


# ==================== 到访提交 ====================

@router.post("/visits/{visit_no}/submit", summary="API-24 提交复查记录并签署")
async def submit_visit(
    visit_no: str,
    payload: VisitSubmitIn,
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:followup:visit")),
):
    """任务完成 + 档案记录点写入 + 判读触发 + 并发症上报（单事务）。"""
    svc = FollowupVisitService(db)
    return success_response(
        await svc.submit(visit_no, payload, operator=user))


# ==================== 屈光进展 ====================

@router.get("/progressions", summary="API-29 预警列表")
async def list_progressions(
    alert_level: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    handled: Optional[bool] = Query(None),
    store_id: Optional[int] = Query(None, ge=0),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=200),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:followup:view")),
):
    """默认按级别倒序、年化进展倒序。"""
    svc = ProgressionService(db)
    return success_response(
        await svc.list_progressions(alert_level=alert_level, handled=handled,
                                    store_id=store_id, page=page,
                                    page_size=page_size, user=user))


@router.post("/progressions/calc", summary="API-30 触发屈光进展判读")
async def calc_progression(
    payload: ProgressionCalcIn,
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:followup:visit")),
):
    """按评估窗口选取起终点并判定预警级别。"""
    svc = ProgressionService(db)
    return success_response(await svc.calc(payload, operator=user))


@router.post("/progressions/{progression_no}/handle", summary="API-32 医生处置预警")
async def handle_progression(
    progression_no: str,
    payload: ProgressionHandleIn,
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:followup:review")),
):
    """处置动作 + 意见 + 复核安排；RATE_ABNORMAL 仅允许 OBSERVE。"""
    svc = ProgressionService(db)
    return success_response(
        await svc.handle(progression_no, payload, operator=user))


def _not_found(code: str, value: str):
    """构造资源不存在异常。"""
    from app.core.exceptions import BizException
    return BizException(code, "资源不存在：%s" % value)
```

```python
# app/api/internal/m10/followup.py
"""M10-09 复查与视力档案 —— 内部契约路由（P-24 / P-25 / P-26）。"""

from datetime import date
from typing import Optional

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Query, Request
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.database import get_db
from app.core.response import success_response
from app.schemas.m10.followup import FollowupPlanCreateIn
from app.services.m10.followup_plan_service import FollowupPlanService
from app.services.m10.followup_task_service import FollowupTaskService
from app.services.m10.vision_archive_service import VisionArchiveService

internal_router = APIRouter(prefix="/internal/v1/m10", tags=["M10-09-内部契约"])


@internal_router.get("/followups/due", summary="P-24 到期复查清单")
async def internal_followups_due(
    date_from: date = Query(..., description="窗口起点"),
    date_to: date = Query(..., description="窗口终点，跨度 ≤366 天"),
    store_id: Optional[int] = Query(None, ge=0),
    followup_type: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="逗号分隔"),
    owner_id: Optional[int] = Query(None, ge=0),
    include_overdue: bool = Query(True),
    only_orthok_dropout: bool = Query(False),
    limit: int = Query(100, ge=1, le=500),
    cursor: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """供 M03-04 / M19 拉取到期复查清单。不落库，不更新触达时间。"""
    svc = FollowupTaskService(db)
    types = [x.strip() for x in (followup_type or "").split(",") if x.strip()]
    result = await svc.list_due(
        date_from=date_from, date_to=date_to, store_id=store_id,
        followup_types=types or None, owner_id=owner_id,
        include_overdue=include_overdue,
        only_orthok_dropout=only_orthok_dropout,
        limit=limit, cursor=cursor,
    )
    return success_response(result)


@internal_router.get(
    "/vision-archive/{patient_id}/summary",
    summary="P-25 视力健康档案摘要",
)
async def internal_archive_summary(
    patient_id: int,
    archive_type: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    window_months: Optional[int] = Query(None, ge=1, le=60),
    include_records: bool = Query(False),
    record_limit: int = Query(12, ge=1, le=60),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """供 M07 / M11 / M22 取档案摘要；未建档返回成功响应且 archive_no 为空。"""
    svc = VisionArchiveService(db)
    result = await svc.get_summary(
        patient_id=patient_id, archive_type=archive_type,
        window_months=window_months, include_records=include_records,
        record_limit=record_limit,
    )
    return success_response(result)


@internal_router.post("/followups/on-created", summary="P-26 复查计划创建通知")
async def internal_on_created(
    payload: FollowupPlanCreateIn,
    request: Request,
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """供 M03-04 / M19 发起复查计划；与 API-06 共用同一创建逻辑。"""
    idem_key = request.headers.get("Idempotency-Key")
    svc = FollowupPlanService(db)
    result = await svc.create_plan(
        payload, idem_key=idem_key, operator=None, source_system="INTERNAL")
    return success_response(result)
```

**路由实现要点：**

| # | 要点 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | **`tasks/blocking/{patient_id}` 前置声明** | 否则 `blocking` 被 `{task_no}` 吞掉；同时 `task_no` 加 `^FT[0-9]{14}$` 正则双保险 |
| 2 | **对外/内部分 router** | `internal_router` 前缀 `/internal/v1/m10`，仅内网网关可达；对外 router 走 `/api/v1/m10/followups` |
| 3 | **P-25 路径用单数 `vision-archive`** | 与共享规范 §8 原文一字不差；对外 API 用复数 `vision-archives`，互不干扰 |
| 4 | **权限装饰器前置** | 每个接口显式 `require_permission`，内部契约由网关 `X-Internal-Token` 校验 |
| 5 | **幂等键统一从请求头取** | Service 层决定是否使用，路由层不做判断 |
| 6 | **P-26 复用 `create_plan`** | 内部与对外两条入口共用同一实现，避免规则分叉 |

### 6.5 Service 层核心逻辑示例

```python
# app/services/m10/followup_plan_service.py
"""M10-09 复查与视力档案 —— 复查计划服务（规则展开 / 计划生成 / 状态机）。

本模块铁律（提醒所有维护者）：
  1. 复查节点由规则展开，规则改版不影响已生成计划（node_snapshot 快照）
  2. 屈光数据一律来自 M10-01，本模块不产生任何验光原始值
  3. 触达前必须过合规闸门（拒收 / 监护人 / 冷却 / 月上限）
  4. 不碰钱、不碰库存
"""

from datetime import date, datetime, timedelta
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

from sqlalchemy import and_, func, select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.config import settings
from app.core.exceptions import BizException
from app.core.redis import redis_client
from app.models.m10.followup import (
    M10FollowupPlan, M10FollowupRule, M10FollowupTask,
)

IDEM_TTL = 86400


class FollowupPlanService:
    """复查规则与计划领域服务。"""

    def __init__(self, db: AsyncSession) -> None:
        self.db = db

    # ==================== 规则展开（核心算法）====================

    def expand_nodes(self, rule: M10FollowupRule, start_date: date) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """把规则展开为带日期的节点列表。

        普通规则：按 node_list.nodes 逐个计算 due_date。
        FR004 / FR005（OK 镜）：D1/D7/D30/D90 之后每 90 天滚动至第 540 天。
        """
        nodes: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        raw = (rule.node_list or {}).get("nodes", [])
        for idx, node in enumerate(raw, start=1):
            offset = int(node["offset_days"])
            nodes.append({
                "seq": idx,
                "node_code": node["node_code"],
                "node_name": node["node_name"],
                "offset_days": offset,
                "items": list(node.get("items", [])),
                "required": bool(node.get("required", True)),
                "due_date": start_date + timedelta(days=offset),
            })

        if rule.rule_code in ("FR004", "FR005"):
            nodes = self._expand_orthok_rolling(rule, nodes, start_date)
        return nodes

    @staticmethod
    def _expand_orthok_rolling(
        rule: M10FollowupRule,
        base_nodes: List[Dict[str, Any]],
        start_date: date,
    ) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """OK 镜滚动展开：D90 之后每 90 天一个节点，至 ORTHOK_LAST_DAY（540）。"""
        last_day = int(settings.M10_FU_ORTHOK_LAST_DAY)
        step = 90
        nodes = list(base_nodes)
        offset = step * 4 // 4 * 90  # 从 D90 之后开始
        offset = 90
        while offset + step <= last_day:
            offset += step
            months = offset // 30
            nodes.append({
                "seq": len(nodes) + 1,
                "node_code": _code_from_months(months),
                "node_name": "第 %d 个月" % months,
                "offset_days": offset,
                "items": ["VA_UNCORRECTED", "REF", "CORNEAL_STAINING", "AXIAL_LENGTH"],
                "required": True,
                "due_date": start_date + timedelta(days=offset),
            })
        if rule.rule_code == "FR005":
            extra: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
            for node in nodes:
                extra.append(node)
                extra.append({
                    "seq": len(extra) + 1,
                    "node_code": "EXTRA",
                    "node_name": "%s 补查" % node["node_name"],
                    "offset_days": int(node["offset_days"]) + 14,
                    "items": ["VA_UNCORRECTED", "CORNEAL_STAINING"],
                    "required": False,
                    "due_date": node["due_date"] + timedelta(days=14),
                })
            return extra
        return nodes

    def build_tasks(self, nodes: List[Dict[str, Any]], rule: M10FollowupRule) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """把节点转换为任务字典，计算提醒日与到访窗口。"""
        before = int(settings.M10_FU_DEFAULT_WINDOW_BEFORE)
        after = int(settings.M10_FU_DEFAULT_WINDOW_AFTER)
        d1_after = int(settings.M10_FU_ORTHOK_D1_WINDOW_AFTER)
        tasks: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        for node in nodes:
            due = node["due_date"]
            window_after = d1_after if node["node_code"] == "D1" else after
            tasks.append({
                "node_code": node["node_code"],
                "node_name": node["node_name"],
                "node_seq": node["seq"],
                "node_items": node["items"],
                "is_required": node["required"],
                "due_date": due,
                "remind_date": due - timedelta(days=int(rule.notify_before_days)),
                "window_start": due - timedelta(days=before),
                "window_end": due + timedelta(days=window_after),
            })
        return tasks

    # ==================== 计划创建 ====================

    async def create_plan(
        self,
        payload: Any,
        idem_key: Optional[str] = None,
        operator: Any = None,
        source_system: str = "MANUAL",
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """创建复查计划并展开任务。幂等键：Idempotency-Key + (来源单类型, 来源单号, 规则)。"""
        # 1. 幂等：Redis 第一道
        idem = idem_key or "%s:%s:%s" % (
            payload.source_type, payload.source_no, payload.rule_code)
        cache_key = "m10:idem:followup_plan:%s" % idem
        hit = await redis_client.get(cache_key)
        if hit:
            import json
            data = json.loads(hit)
            data["is_idempotent"] = True
            return data

        # 2. 规则校验
        rule = await self._get_enabled_rule(payload.rule_code)
        if rule is None:
            raise BizException("1009001", "复查规则不存在或已停用",
                               data={"rule_code": payload.rule_code})

        # 3. 患者与年龄
        patient = await self._get_patient(payload.patient_id)
        age = _calc_age(patient.get("birth_date"))
        if not _scope_matches(rule.patient_scope, age):
            raise BizException("1009002", "规则适用人群与患者年龄不匹配",
                               data={"patient_scope": rule.patient_scope, "age": age})

        # 4. 未成年人必须有监护人电话
        if age < int(settings.M10_FU_MINOR_AGE) and not patient.get("guardian_phone"):
            raise BizException("1009005", "未成年患者缺少监护人联系方式",
                               data={"patient_id": payload.patient_id})

        # 5. 来源单据校验
        if payload.source_type != "MANUAL":
            await self._check_source(payload.source_type, payload.source_no,
                                     payload.patient_id)

        # 6. 同来源同规则未终结计划
        exist = await self._find_open_plan(payload.source_type, payload.source_no,
                                           payload.rule_code)
        if exist:
            raise BizException("1009003", "同来源同规则已存在未终结计划",
                               data={"existing_plan_no": exist.plan_no})

        # 7. 展开节点并校验单调性
        start = payload.start_date or date.today()
        nodes = self.expand_nodes(rule, start)
        if not nodes:
            raise BizException("1009004", "节点展开失败：节点为空")
        offsets = [n["offset_days"] for n in nodes]
        if offsets != sorted(offsets):
            raise BizException("1009004", "节点展开失败：日期非单调递增")

        # 8. 落库：计划 + 任务
        plan_no = await _next_no("FC", 4)
        plan = M10FollowupPlan(
            plan_no=plan_no,
            plan_series_no="%s-01" % plan_no.replace("FC", "FCS"),
            patient_id=payload.patient_id,
            patient_name=patient.get("patient_name"),
            gender=patient.get("gender"),
            birth_date=patient.get("birth_date"),
            age_at_plan=age,
            phone=patient.get("phone"),
            guardian_name=patient.get("guardian_name"),
            guardian_phone=patient.get("guardian_phone"),
            followup_type=payload.followup_type,
            rule_code=rule.rule_code,
            rule_version=rule.rule_version,
            node_snapshot={"nodes": nodes, "is_blocking_renew": rule.is_blocking_renew},
            eye_side=getattr(payload, "eye_side", "NA") or "NA",
            source_type=payload.source_type,
            source_no=payload.source_no,
            owner_role=rule.default_owner_role,
            owner_id=getattr(payload, "owner_id", None),
            start_date=start,
            expected_end_date=nodes[-1]["due_date"],
            series_total=len(nodes),
            series_done=0,
            series_missed=0,
            status="PLANNED",
            next_due_date=nodes[0]["due_date"],
            notify_channels=",".join(getattr(payload, "notify_channels", None)
                                     or [rule.notify_channels]),
            notify_enabled=True,
        )
        self.db.add(plan)
        await self.db.flush()

        tasks_out: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        for spec in self.build_tasks(nodes, rule):
            task_no = await _next_no("FT", 6)
            task = M10FollowupTask(
                task_no=task_no,
                plan_id=plan.id,
                plan_no=plan_no,
                plan_series_no=plan.plan_series_no,
                patient_id=plan.patient_id,
                patient_name=plan.patient_name,
                node_code=spec["node_code"],
                node_name=spec["node_name"],
                node_seq=spec["node_seq"],
                node_items=spec["node_items"],
                is_required=spec["is_required"],
                due_date=spec["due_date"],
                remind_date=spec["remind_date"],
                window_start=spec["window_start"],
                window_end=spec["window_end"],
                overdue_days=0,
                owner_role=plan.owner_role,
                owner_id=plan.owner_id,
                status="PLANNED",
                notify_count=0,
            )
            self.db.add(task)
            tasks_out.append({
                "task_no": task_no,
                "node_code": spec["node_code"],
                "node_name": spec["node_name"],
                "due_date": spec["due_date"].isoformat(),
                "window_start": spec["window_start"].isoformat(),
                "window_end": spec["window_end"].isoformat(),
            })

        await self.db.commit()

        result = {
            "plan_no": plan_no,
            "plan_series_no": plan.plan_series_no,
            "patient_id": plan.patient_id,
            "followup_type": plan.followup_type,
            "rule_code": rule.rule_code,
            "rule_version": rule.rule_version,
            "status": plan.status,
            "start_date": start.isoformat(),
            "series_total": plan.series_total,
            "expected_end_date": plan.expected_end_date.isoformat(),
            "next_due_date": plan.next_due_date.isoformat(),
            "tasks": tasks_out[:5],
            "is_idempotent": False,
        }
        await redis_client.setex(cache_key, IDEM_TTL, _dumps(result))
        await self._publish_created(plan, tasks_out)
        return result

    # ==================== 状态机 ====================

    async def terminate(self, plan_no: str, payload: Dict[str, Any],
                        operator: Any = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """终止计划：未终结任务全部置 CANCELLED；OK 镜停戴同步解除阻断。"""
        plan = await self._get_plan(plan_no)
        if plan.status not in ("PLANNED", "ACTIVE"):
            raise BizException("1009008", "计划状态不允许终止",
                               data={"status": plan.status})
        reason = (payload.get("reason") or "").strip()
        if len(reason) < 10:
            raise BizException("1009009", "终止原因必填且不少于 10 字")

        terminated = await self._cancel_open_tasks(plan.id, "PLAN_TERMINATED", reason)
        plan.status = "TERMINATED"
        plan.terminated_reason = reason
        plan.completed_at = datetime.now()
        await self.db.commit()

        result = {
            "plan_no": plan_no,
            "status": "TERMINATED",
            "terminated_tasks": terminated,
            "series_done": plan.series_done,
            "series_missed": plan.series_missed,
            "terminated_at": plan.completed_at.isoformat(),
        }
        await self._audit("PLAN_TERMINATE", result, operator)
        return result

    async def _maybe_complete(self, plan: M10FollowupPlan) -> None:
        """任务完成或漏复查后汇总：全部节点终结则计划转 COMPLETED。"""
        if plan.series_done + plan.series_missed >= plan.series_total:
            plan.status = "COMPLETED"
            plan.completed_at = datetime.now()
        else:
            plan.next_due_date = await self._next_due(plan.id)

    # ==================== 工具 ====================

    @staticmethod
    def calc_equivalence_sphere(sph: Optional[Decimal],
                                cyl: Optional[Decimal]) -> Optional[Decimal]:
        """等效球镜 SE = SPH + CYL / 2，ROUND_HALF_UP 保留 2 位。"""
        if sph is None:
            return None
        c = cyl if cyl is not None else Decimal("0")
        return (Decimal(sph) + Decimal(c) / Decimal("2")).quantize(
            Decimal("0.01"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)

    async def _get_enabled_rule(self, rule_code: str) -> Optional[M10FollowupRule]:
        """取启用且有效期覆盖今天的规则。"""
        raise NotImplementedError

    async def _get_patient(self, patient_id: int) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """调 M03-01 取患者与监护人信息。"""
        raise NotImplementedError

    async def _check_source(self, source_type: str, source_no: str,
                            patient_id: int) -> None:
        """校验来源单据存在且属于该患者。"""
        raise NotImplementedError

    async def _find_open_plan(self, source_type: str, source_no: str,
                              rule_code: str) -> Optional[M10FollowupPlan]:
        """查同来源同规则的未终结计划。"""
        raise NotImplementedError

    async def _get_plan(self, plan_no: str) -> M10FollowupPlan:
        """按计划号取计划。"""
        raise NotImplementedError

    async def _cancel_open_tasks(self, plan_id: int, reason_code: str,
                                 reason: str) -> int:
        """把计划下未终结任务置 CANCELLED。"""
        raise NotImplementedError

    async def _next_due(self, plan_id: int) -> Optional[date]:
        """取剩余任务中最早的到期日。"""
        raise NotImplementedError

    async def _publish_created(self, plan: M10FollowupPlan,
                               tasks: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> None:
        """发布 m10.followup.created 事件。"""
        raise NotImplementedError

    async def _audit(self, action: str, payload: Dict[str, Any],
                     operator: Any) -> None:
        """写审计日志。"""
        raise NotImplementedError


def _code_from_months(months: int) -> str:
    """把月数映射为节点编码：3 -> M3, 18 -> M18。"""
    return "M%d" % months


def _calc_age(birth_date: Optional[date]) -> int:
    """按出生日期算周岁；未知返回 0。"""
    if not birth_date:
        return 0
    today = date.today()
    return today.year - birth_date.year - (
        (today.month, today.day) < (birth_date.month, birth_date.day))


def _scope_matches(scope: str, age: int) -> bool:
    """规则适用人群判定：ALL / CHILD / ADULT。"""
    if scope == "ALL":
        return True
    if scope == "CHILD":
        return age < int(settings.M10_FU_MINOR_AGE)
    if scope == "ADULT":
        return age >= int(settings.M10_FU_MINOR_AGE)
    return True


def _dumps(value: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    """JSON 序列化（Decimal/date 转字符串）。"""
    import json
    return json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, default=str)


async def _next_no(prefix: str, seq_len: int) -> str:
    """生成业务单号：Redis INCR + 日期前缀。"""
    raise NotImplementedError
```

```python
# app/services/m10/progression_service.py
"""M10-09 复查与视力档案 —— 屈光进展判读服务。

判定顺序（短路）：
  ① 观察期保护 → RATE_ABNORMAL / LOW
  ② SE 与眼轴双高危 → RAPID_COMBINED / CRITICAL
  ③ SE 年化 → PROGRESSION / CRITICAL|HIGH|MEDIUM|LOW
  ④ 眼轴增速 → AXIAL / HIGH|MEDIUM|LOW
"""

from datetime import date
from decimal import ROUND_HALF_UP, Decimal
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.config import settings
from app.core.exceptions import BizException

TWO = Decimal("0.01")


class ProgressionService:
    """屈光进展判读与预警处置。"""

    def __init__(self, db: AsyncSession) -> None:
        self.db = db

    # ==================== 核心判定 ====================

    def judge(
        self,
        base_se: Decimal,
        curr_se: Decimal,
        base_axial: Optional[Decimal],
        curr_axial: Optional[Decimal],
        interval_months: Decimal,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """按三级阈值判定预警级别（纯函数，便于单测覆盖）。"""
        min_interval = Decimal(str(settings.M10_FU_MIN_INTERVAL_MONTHS))
        se_watch = Decimal(str(settings.M10_FU_SE_RATE_WATCH))
        se_warning = Decimal(str(settings.M10_FU_SE_RATE_WARNING))
        se_high = Decimal(str(settings.M10_FU_SE_RATE_HIGH))
        ax_warning = Decimal(str(settings.M10_FU_AXIAL_RATE_WARNING))
        ax_high = Decimal(str(settings.M10_FU_AXIAL_RATE_HIGH))

        delta_se = (Decimal(curr_se) - Decimal(base_se)).quantize(TWO, rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)
        rate = (delta_se * Decimal("12") / interval_months).quantize(
            TWO, rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP) if interval_months else None

        axial_rate = None
        if base_axial is not None and curr_axial is not None and interval_months:
            delta_ax = Decimal(curr_axial) - Decimal(base_axial)
            axial_rate = (delta_ax * Decimal("12") / interval_months).quantize(
                TWO, rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)

        # ① 观察期保护：绝不能因为小样本判高危
        if interval_months < min_interval:
            return {
                "delta_se": delta_se,
                "rate_per_year": rate,
                "axial_growth_rate": axial_rate,
                "alert_type": "RATE_ABNORMAL",
                "alert_level": "LOW",
                "need_intervention": False,
                "review_required": False,
                "min_interval_ok": False,
            }

        abs_rate = abs(rate) if rate is not None else Decimal("0")
        ax = axial_rate or Decimal("0")

        # ② 双高危联合
        if abs_rate >= se_high and ax >= ax_high:
            return self._result(delta_se, rate, axial_rate, "RAPID_COMBINED",
                                "CRITICAL", True, True)

        # ③ SE 年化
        if abs_rate >= se_high:
            return self._result(delta_se, rate, axial_rate, "PROGRESSION",
                                "CRITICAL", True, True)
        if abs_rate >= se_warning:
            return self._result(delta_se, rate, axial_rate, "PROGRESSION",
                                "HIGH", False, True)
        if abs_rate >= se_watch:
            return self._result(delta_se, rate, axial_rate, "PROGRESSION",
                                "MEDIUM", False, False)

        # ④ 眼轴增速
        if axial_rate is not None:
            if ax >= ax_high:
                return self._result(delta_se, rate, axial_rate, "AXIAL",
                                    "HIGH", False, True)
            if ax >= ax_warning:
                return self._result(delta_se, rate, axial_rate, "AXIAL",
                                    "MEDIUM", False, False)
            if ax >= Decimal("0.20"):
                return self._result(delta_se, rate, axial_rate, "AXIAL",
                                    "LOW", False, False)

        return self._result(delta_se, rate, axial_rate, "PROGRESSION", "LOW", False, False)

    @staticmethod
    def _result(delta_se: Decimal, rate: Optional[Decimal],
                axial_rate: Optional[Decimal], alert_type: str,
                alert_level: str, need_intervention: bool,
                review_required: bool) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """组装判定结果。"""
        return {
            "delta_se": delta_se,
            "rate_per_year": rate,
            "axial_growth_rate": axial_rate,
            "alert_type": alert_type,
            "alert_level": alert_level,
            "need_intervention": need_intervention,
            "review_required": review_required,
            "min_interval_ok": True,
        }

    # ==================== 判读入口 ====================

    async def calc(self, payload: Any, operator: Any = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """触发判读：取起终点、算年化、判定、落库、推送。"""
        archive = await self._get_archive(payload.archive_no)
        base, curr = await self._pick_records(
            archive, payload.base_record_id, payload.curr_record_id,
            payload.window_months)

        if base is None or curr is None or base["id"] == curr["id"]:
            raise BizException("1009026", "档案缺少可比对的记录点",
                               data={"archive_no": payload.archive_no})

        interval = _months_between(base["record_date"], curr["record_date"])
        se_base = _avg(base["se_od"], base["se_os"])
        se_curr = _avg(curr["se_od"], curr["se_os"])
        ax_base = _avg(base.get("axial_length_od"), base.get("axial_length_os"))
        ax_curr = _avg(curr.get("axial_length_od"), curr.get("axial_length_os"))

        judged = self.judge(se_base, se_curr, ax_base, ax_curr, interval)
        progression_no = await _save_progression(
            archive, base, curr, interval, judged,
            threshold_version=int(settings.M10_FU_THRESHOLD_VERSION))

        if judged["alert_level"] in ("HIGH", "CRITICAL"):
            await self._publish_alert(progression_no, judged)
        await self._refresh_archive_cache(archive, judged)
        return {
            "archive_no": payload.archive_no,
            "progression_no": progression_no,
            "interval_months": float(interval),
            "se_rate_per_year": float(judged["rate_per_year"]) if judged["rate_per_year"] else None,
            "axial_growth_rate": (float(judged["axial_growth_rate"])
                                  if judged["axial_growth_rate"] else None),
            "alert_level": judged["alert_level"],
            "alert_type": judged["alert_type"],
            "need_intervention": judged["need_intervention"],
            "review_required": judged["review_required"],
            "is_idempotent": False,
        }

    async def handle(self, progression_no: str, payload: Any,
                     operator: Any = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """医生处置：校验后写入处置记录并推送家长。"""
        row = await self._get_progression(progression_no)
        if row.get("handled_at"):
            raise BizException("1009030", "该预警已被处置",
                               data={"handled_at": str(row["handled_at"])})

        action = payload.handle_action
        if action == "REFER" and not getattr(payload, "refer_dept_id", None):
            raise BizException("1009031", "转诊必须填写转诊科室")
        if row["alert_level"] in ("HIGH", "CRITICAL"):
            if len((payload.handle_remark or "").strip()) < 10:
                raise BizException("1009031", "高危预警处置意见必填且不少于 10 字")
        if getattr(payload, "review_required", False) and not getattr(
                payload, "next_review_date", None):
            raise BizException("1009031", "要求复核时必须填写下次复查日期")
        # 观察期不足的记录只允许观察，防止小样本驱动干预
        if row["alert_type"] == "RATE_ABNORMAL" and action != "OBSERVE":
            raise BizException("1009032", "观察期不足的记录不允许干预类处置",
                               data={"alert_type": "RATE_ABNORMAL"})

        await self._save_handle(progression_no, payload, operator)
        notified, notify_no = await self._notify_parent(row, payload)
        return {
            "progression_no": progression_no,
            "handled_at": _now(),
            "handle_action": action,
            "need_intervention": bool(getattr(payload, "need_intervention", False)),
            "next_review_date": getattr(payload, "next_review_date", None),
            "notified": notified,
            "notify_no": notify_no,
        }

    # ==================== 仓储声明 ====================

    async def _get_archive(self, archive_no: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """取档案。"""
        raise NotImplementedError

    async def _pick_records(self, archive: Dict[str, Any],
                            base_id: Optional[int], curr_id: Optional[int],
                            window_months: Optional[int]):
        """按评估窗口选取起点与终点记录。"""
        raise NotImplementedError

    async def _get_progression(self, progression_no: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """取判读记录。"""
        raise NotImplementedError

    async def _publish_alert(self, progression_no: str, judged: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """发布 m10.vision.alert 事件。"""
        raise NotImplementedError

    async def _refresh_archive_cache(self, archive: Dict[str, Any],
                                     judged: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """刷新档案冗余字段与摘要缓存。"""
        raise NotImplementedError

    async def _save_handle(self, progression_no: str, payload: Any,
                           operator: Any) -> None:
        """写入处置结果。"""
        raise NotImplementedError

    async def _notify_parent(self, row: Dict[str, Any], payload: Any):
        """通知家长（过触达闸门）。"""
        raise NotImplementedError


def _months_between(start: date, end: date) -> Decimal:
    """计算间隔月数（保留 1 位小数）：年份差×12 + 月份差 + 天数差/30。"""
    months = (end.year - start.year) * 12 + (end.month - start.month)
    months += (end.day - start.day) / 30.0
    return Decimal(str(months)).quantize(Decimal("0.1"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)


def _avg(a: Optional[Decimal], b: Optional[Decimal]) -> Optional[Decimal]:
    """双眼平均；任一为空则返回另一只；都空返回 None。"""
    if a is None and b is None:
        return None
    if a is None:
        return Decimal(b)
    if b is None:
        return Decimal(a)
    return ((Decimal(a) + Decimal(b)) / Decimal("2")).quantize(
        TWO, rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)


def _now() -> str:
    """当前时间 ISO 字符串。"""
    from datetime import datetime
    return datetime.now().isoformat()


async def _save_progression(archive, base, curr, interval, judged, threshold_version):
    """落库判读记录（幂等键：档案 + 起终点 + 阈值版本）。"""
    raise NotImplementedError
```

**Service 层设计要点：**

| # | 要点 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | **`expand_nodes` 与 `build_tasks` 分离** | 节点展开（业务语义）与窗口计算（配置驱动）分开，便于单测与配置调整 |
| 2 | **`FR004/FR005` 滚动展开单独实现** | OK 镜节点不是固定列表，用 `while offset + 90 <= 540` 生成，`FR005` 额外插入 +14 天补查节点 |
| 3 | **`judge()` 是纯函数** | 不碰数据库，输入输出全为数值，可用参数化单测穷举八档阈值边界 |
| 4 | **观察期保护写在判定第一步** | 任何后续判定都不会覆盖 `RATE_ABNORMAL`，从代码结构上杜绝「小样本判高危」 |
| 5 | **`handle()` 拒绝 RATE_ABNORMAL 的干预** | 与 P07 交互规则一致，防止误判驱动临床决策 |
| 6 | **`node_snapshot` 隔离规则改版** | 计划创建时冻结节点，之后规则怎么改都不影响已生成计划 |
| 7 | **`_maybe_complete` 汇总完成** | 计划完成不是「最后一个任务完成」触发，而是「已终结数 = 总数」，覆盖漏复查场景 |
| 8 | **仓储方法声明 `NotImplementedError`** | 文档聚焦业务逻辑，实现按仓储模式拆分到 `app/repositories/m10/` |

---
## 7. 缓存与异步任务设计

### 7.1 Redis 缓存设计

Redis Key 统一在本模块域 `m10:fu:*` 与 `m10:vision:*` 内（共享规范 §14 允许 `m10:followup:*`，本模块用 `m10:fu:*` 作短前缀以节省内存，映射关系在 Key 前缀表中注明）。

| Key 模式 | 类型 | TTL | 说明 | 失效时机 | 容量估算 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `m10:fu:rule:{rule_code}` | String(JSON) | 1800s | 单条规则详情（含 node_list） | 规则修改后 `DEL` | 8 × 4KB ≈ 32KB |
| `m10:fu:rules:{trigger_type}` | String(JSON) | 600s | 规则列表 | 同上 | 6 × 8KB ≈ 48KB |
| `m10:fu:plan:{plan_no}` | String(JSON) | 300s | 计划详情快照 | 计划变更/任务状态变更 | 2,400 × 3KB ≈ 7MB |
| `m10:fu:task:{task_no}` | String(JSON) | 180s | 任务详情 | 任务状态变更 | 峰值 2,000 × 1KB ≈ 2MB |
| `m10:fu:duequeue:{store_id}:{yyyyMMdd}` | String(JSON) | 600s | P-24 到期清单（按门店日缓存） | 每日 00:05 任务重建 | 10 × 200KB ≈ 2MB |
| `m10:fu:notifygate:{patient_id}` | Hash | 86400s | 触达闸门计数（本日月次数、上次触达时间） | 自然过期（按日重置） | 6,000 × 200B ≈ 1.2MB |
| `m10:fu:refused:{patient_id}` | String | 永久（无 TTL） | 拒收静默名单（0 次自动触达） | 患者重新授权时 `DEL` | 约 200 条 |
| `m10:fu:stat:{period}:{store_id}:{type}` | String(JSON) | 300s | 依从性统计卡片 | 统计重算后 `DEL` | 60 × 20KB ≈ 1.2MB |
| `m10:fu:workbench:{store_id}:{date}:{user_id}` | String(JSON) | 60s | P01 工作台聚合 | 自然过期 | 30 × 5KB ≈ 150KB |
| `m10:vision:archive:{archive_no}` | String(JSON) | 600s | 档案详情（不含记录点） | 记录点新增/作废 | 6,000 × 2KB ≈ 12MB |
| `m10:vision:summary:{patient_id}:{archive_no}` | String(JSON) | 300s | **P-25 摘要缓存（核心）** | 记录点变更、判读更新 | 峰值 1,500 × 4KB ≈ 6MB |
| `m10:vision:progress:{archive_id}:{tag}` | String(JSON) | 600s | 判读结果缓存 | 判读重算后 `DEL` | 6,000 × 2KB ≈ 12MB |
| `m10:vision:curve:{archive_no}` | String(JSON) | 600s | 曲线点数组（P06/P07 画图） | 记录点变更 | 6,000 × 6KB ≈ 36MB |
| `m10:idem:followup_plan:{key}` | String(JSON) | 86400s | 计划创建幂等键 | 自然过期 | 峰值 300 × 2KB |
| `m10:idem:followup_notify:{key}` | String(JSON) | 86400s | 触达幂等键 | 自然过期 | 峰值 1,000 × 500B |
| `m10:lock:followup_plan:{key}` | String | 10s | 计划生成互斥 | 释放或超时 | 峰值 20 |
| `m10:lock:fu_scan:{scan_name}` | String | 1800s | 定时扫描互斥（防多实例重复跑） | 释放或超时 | 12 |
| `m10:fu:seq:{prefix}:{yyyyMMdd}` | String | 172800s | 单号计数器 | 自然过期 | 9 |

**缓存总容量估算：** 约 80MB（Redis 实例 512MB，占用率 15.6%，安全）。

**三类缓存防护：**

| 风险 | 场景 | 防护 |
|---|---|---|
| **穿透** | 大量查询不存在的 `archive_no` | 空结果缓存 60 秒（`{"__null__": true}`） |
| **击穿** | `m10:vision:summary:*` 热门档案缓存同时过期 | 重建用 `SET NX m10:lock:vs:{archive_no}`，其余请求等 100ms 后读缓存 |
| **雪崩** | 统计重算后批量失效 | TTL 加 ±10% 抖动；批量失效分批 100 个、间隔 50ms |

**触达闸门计数器设计（合规的关键实现）：**

```
Key:  m10:fu:notifygate:{patient_id}   Type: Hash
Fields:
  last_remind_at      最近一次提醒类触达时间（ISO 字符串）
  last_recall_at      最近一次召回类触达时间
  month_counter       Hash 字段 month = 'YYYY-MM'，值为当月触达次数
  month_since         当前计数所属月份

判定逻辑（发送前）：
  1. 读 REFUSED 名单 → 命中且 channel != PHONE → 拦截
  2. month_since != 当前月 → HINCRBY 重置计数为 0
  3. HGET month_counter >= MONTHLY_NOTIFY_CAP → 拦截
  4. now - last_*_at < NOTIFY_COOLDOWN_DAYS → 拦截
  5. 全部通过 → 发送成功后 HINCRBY month_counter 1，HSET last_*_at

为什么用 Redis 而不是每次都查 DB：
  发送高峰（每日 9:00~9:10）批量触达 300+ 条，逐条查 DB 统计
  当月次数需要扫 m10_followup_notify（月 3,000 行），单条 3ms，
  累计 900ms，会拖慢批处理。Redis 单次 HGET < 1ms。
  DB 仍是权威口径，Redis 仅作计数器，每日 03:20 由
  job_followup_notify_counter_reconcile 与 DB 对账并修正。
```

### 7.2 Celery 异步任务

```python
# app/tasks/m10/followup_tasks.py
"""M10-09 复查与视力档案 —— Celery 异步任务。"""

import asyncio

from celery import shared_task


@shared_task(name="m10.followup.archive_on_signed")
def archive_on_signed_task(payload):
    """验光签发事件 → 生成/更新档案记录点。

    唯一数据源是 M10-01 验光单；本任务只做快照抽取，不重算验光值。
    """
    return asyncio.run(_archive_on_signed(payload))


async def _archive_on_signed(payload):
    """抽取屈光值写入 m10_vision_archive_record，并触发判读。"""
    from app.core.database import AsyncSessionLocal
    from app.services.m10.vision_archive_service import VisionArchiveService

    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
        svc = VisionArchiveService(db)
        return await svc.append_from_optometry(payload)


@shared_task(name="m10.followup.progression_calc")
def progression_calc_task(archive_no, window_months=None):
    """异步计算屈光进展（避免在到访提交事务内做重计算）。"""
    return asyncio.run(_progress_calc(archive_no, window_months))


async def _progress_calc(archive_no, window_months):
    """取档案与记录点，按阈值判定并落库。"""
    from app.core.database import AsyncSessionLocal
    from app.services.m10.progression_service import ProgressionService

    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
        svc = ProgressionService(db)
        payload = _build_calc_payload(archive_no, window_months)
        return await svc.calc(payload, operator=None)


@shared_task(name="m10.followup.complication_report", bind=True, max_retries=5)
def complication_report_task(self, visit_no, payload):
    """并发症上报 M10-04；失败必须重试，绝不允许静默丢弃。"""
    try:
        return asyncio.run(_report_complication(visit_no, payload))
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        raise self.retry(exc=exc, countdown=60)


async def _report_complication(visit_no, payload):
    """调 M10-04 并发症接口；成功后标记已上报。"""
    from app.clients.m10.orthok_client import OrthoKClient
    from app.core.database import AsyncSessionLocal
    from app.services.m10.followup_visit_service import FollowupVisitService

    client = OrthoKClient()
    result = await client.report_complication(payload)
    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
        svc = FollowupVisitService(db)
        await svc.mark_complication_reported(visit_no, result)
    return result


@shared_task(name="m10.followup.report_batch")
def report_batch_task(task_id, archive_nos, report_type, template, print_flag):
    """批量生成档案报告（成长报告 / 进展摘要）。"""
    return asyncio.run(_report_batch(task_id, archive_nos, report_type, template, print_flag))


async def _report_batch(task_id, archive_nos, report_type, template, print_flag):
    """逐个生成，失败记录原因，完成后回写任务状态。"""
    from app.core.database import AsyncSessionLocal
    from app.services.m10.vision_report_service import VisionReportService

    ok, failed = 0, []
    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
        svc = VisionReportService(db)
        for archive_no in archive_nos:
            try:
                await svc.build_report(archive_no, report_type, template)
                ok += 1
            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
                failed.append({"archive_no": archive_no, "reason": str(exc)})
        await svc.finish_batch(task_id, ok, failed, print_flag)
    return {"task_id": task_id, "success": ok, "failed": failed}


@shared_task(name="m10.followup.stat_rebuild")
def stat_rebuild_task(task_id, stat_period, date_from, date_to, store_id):
    """重算指定区间依从性统计。"""
    return asyncio.run(_stat_rebuild(task_id, stat_period, date_from, date_to, store_id))


async def _stat_rebuild(task_id, stat_period, date_from, date_to, store_id):
    """按日期逐日重算，ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE 覆盖。"""
    from app.core.database import AsyncSessionLocal
    from app.services.m10.followup_stat_service import FollowupStatService

    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
        svc = FollowupStatService(db)
        return await svc.rebuild(task_id, stat_period, date_from, date_to, store_id)


@shared_task(name="m10.followup.orphan_plan_scan")
def orphan_plan_scan_task():
    """补扫「已交付订单 / 已发放 OK 镜 但无复查计划」的遗漏。"""
    return asyncio.run(_orphan_scan())


async def _orphan_scan():
    """这是 OK 镜医疗安全的兜底：漏建计划必须被发现。"""
    from app.core.database import AsyncSessionLocal
    from app.services.m10.followup_reconcile_service import FollowupReconcileService

    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
        svc = FollowupReconcileService(db)
        return await svc.scan_orphan_plans(days=30)


def _build_calc_payload(archive_no, window_months):
    """构造判读入参（内部使用）。"""
    class _Payload:
        pass

    p = _Payload()
    p.archive_no = archive_no
    p.base_record_id = None
    p.curr_record_id = None
    p.window_months = window_months
    p.force = False
    return p
```

**Celery 任务清单：**

| 任务名 | 触发 | 用途 | 超时 | 重试 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `m10.followup.archive_on_signed` | `m10.optometry.signed` 事件 | 验光签发 → 档案记录点 | 60s | 5 次，退避 10/30/60/120/300s |
| `m10.followup.progression_calc` | 记录点新增 / 手工触发 | 异步判读 | 30s | 3 次，退避 30s |
| `m10.followup.complication_report` | 复查提交含并发症 | 上报 M10-04 | 30s | **5 次，退避 60s（不允许丢弃）** |
| `m10.followup.report_batch` | 用户批量打印 | 报告生成 | 1800s | 不重试（可重发） |
| `m10.followup.stat_rebuild` | 用户重算 | 统计重算 | 900s | 2 次 |
| `m10.followup.orphan_plan_scan` | 每日 02:30 | 漏建计划补扫 | 300s | 3 次 |

### 7.3 APScheduler 定时任务

| 任务名 | Cron | 说明 | 互斥锁 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `job_followup_remind_scan` | `0 9 * * *` | 扫描 `remind_date = today` 的任务，生成待提醒队列并发送首提醒 | `m10:lock:fu_scan:remind` |
| `job_followup_recall_scan` | `20 9 * * *` | 扫描逾期任务，按 `recall_intervals` 计算应发第几次召回 | `m10:lock:fu_scan:recall` |
| `job_followup_missed_scan` | `0 1 * * *` | `today > window_end` 且状态 `PLANNED`/`NOTIFIED` → `MISSED`，写 `miss_reason = PATIENT_NO_SHOW` | `m10:lock:fu_scan:missed` |
| `job_followup_overdue_alert` | `10 1 * * *` | 逾期 > `M10.FU.ORTHOK_LOCK_DAYS` 的计划标脱管，发 `m10.followup.overdue`，推送护士 | `m10:lock:fu_scan:overdue` |
| `job_followup_plan_activate` | `0 0 * * *` | 首个任务 `remind_date <= today` 的 `PLANNED` 计划 → `ACTIVE` | `m10:lock:fu_scan:activate` |
| `job_followup_plan_complete` | `30 1 * * *` | 已终结节点数 = 总数 的 `ACTIVE` 计划 → `COMPLETED` | `m10:lock:fu_scan:complete` |
| `job_followup_notify_retry` | `*/15 * * * *` | 重发 `send_status = FAILED` 且 `retry_count < 上限` 的触达 | `m10:lock:fu_scan:notify_retry` |
| `job_followup_notify_counter_reconcile` | `20 3 * * *` | Redis 触达计数与 DB 对账并修正 | `m10:lock:fu_scan:counter` |
| `job_followup_stat_daily` | `50 0 * * *` | 计算昨日依从性统计（`DAY` 粒度） | `m10:lock:fu_scan:stat` |
| `job_followup_stat_monthly` | `20 1 2 * *` | 计算上月统计（`MONTH` 粒度） | 同上 |
| `job_followup_stat_finalize` | `40 2 * * *` | D+7 后把统计置 `is_final = TRUE` | 同上 |
| `job_followup_orphan_plan_scan` | `30 2 * * *` | 漏建计划补扫（调 Celery） | `m10:lock:fu_scan:orphan` |
| `job_followup_archive_consistency` | `10 2 * * *` | 档案记录点与验光单状态一致性核对 | `m10:lock:fu_scan:archive` |
| `job_followup_progression_recalc` | `0 4 * * 1` | 每周一对近 30 天有记录的档案重算判读（阈值可能已更新） | `m10:lock:fu_scan:prog` |
| `job_followup_critical_escalate` | `0 */2 * * *` | `CRITICAL` 未处置 > 48 小时 → 升级推送给科主任 | `m10:lock:fu_scan:escalate` |
| `job_followup_task_archive` | `0 3 1 * *` | 终态任务按季度归档至 `_hist` 表 | `m10:lock:fu_scan:archive_task` |

```python
# app/jobs/m10/followup_jobs.py
"""M10-09 复查与视力档案 —— APScheduler 定时任务。"""

import logging
from datetime import date, datetime

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


async def job_followup_recall_scan() -> dict:
    """每日 09:20 召回扫描。

    逾期任务按 recall_intervals（默认 3,7 天）计算应发第几次召回，
    并逐条过合规闸门；被拦截的写 SKIPPED 保留合规证据。
    """
    from app.core.database import AsyncSessionLocal
    from app.services.m10.followup_notify_service import FollowupNotifyService

    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
        svc = FollowupNotifyService(db)
        candidates = await svc.find_recall_candidates(date.today())
        sent, blocked, failed = 0, 0, 0
        for cand in candidates:
            try:
                result = await svc.send_one(cand, notify_type=cand["recall_type"])
                if result["send_status"] == "SENT":
                    sent += 1
                elif result["send_status"] == "SKIPPED":
                    blocked += 1
                else:
                    failed += 1
            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
                failed += 1
                logger.warning("recall_send_failed task=%s err=%s",
                               cand.get("task_no"), exc)
        return {"candidates": len(candidates), "sent": sent,
                "blocked": blocked, "failed": failed}


async def job_followup_missed_scan() -> dict:
    """每日 01:00 漏复查判定：窗口结束后仍未到访 → MISSED。"""
    from app.core.database import AsyncSessionLocal
    from app.services.m10.followup_task_service import FollowupTaskService

    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
        svc = FollowupTaskService(db)
        count = await svc.mark_missed(date.today())
        return {"missed": count}


async def job_followup_overdue_alert() -> dict:
    """每日 01:10 脱管判定与告警。

    逾期超过 M10.FU.ORTHOK_LOCK_DAYS（默认 30 天）且 is_blocking_renew
    为真的计划计为脱管，发布 m10.followup.overdue 并推送护士跟进。
    """
    from app.core.database import AsyncSessionLocal
    from app.services.m10.followup_task_service import FollowupTaskService

    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
        svc = FollowupTaskService(db)
        dropouts = await svc.find_dropouts(date.today())
        for row in dropouts:
            logger.error(
                "orthok_dropout plan_no=%s patient_id=%s overdue_days=%s",
                row["plan_no"], row["patient_id"], row["overdue_days"])
        await svc.publish_overdue_events(dropouts)
        return {"dropout": len(dropouts)}


async def job_followup_archive_consistency() -> dict:
    """每日 02:10 档案一致性核对。

    检查 is_effective = TRUE 的记录点其来源验光单是否已作废；
    不一致则置记录点无效并告警（这是「不建第二套验光表」的对账保障）。
    """
    from app.core.database import AsyncSessionLocal
    from app.services.m10.followup_reconcile_service import FollowupReconcileService

    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
        svc = FollowupReconcileService(db)
        diffs = await svc.check_archive_consistency()
        for row in diffs:
            logger.error("archive_record_inconsistent record_no=%s optometry_no=%s",
                         row["record_no"], row["optometry_no"])
        fixed = await svc.fix_archive_consistency(diffs)
        return {"inconsistent": len(diffs), "fixed": fixed}
```

**定时任务排序原则：**

```
00:00  计划 PLANNED → ACTIVE
01:00  漏复查判定（MISSED）
01:10  脱管判定与告警
01:30  计划完成汇总（COMPLETED）
02:10  档案一致性核对
02:30  漏建计划补扫
02:40  统计定稿（D+7）
03:00  统计日粒度计算
03:20  触达计数对账
04:00  周一：判读批量重算
09:00  首提醒扫描（不早于 9 点，避免打扰）
09:20  召回扫描（一轮完成后错峰）
每 15 分钟：触达失败重试
每 2 小时：CRITICAL 未处置升级
```

> **为什么提醒扫描排到 9 点？** 短信/微信提醒若在凌晨发送，会被大量用户视为骚扰并触发投诉与拉黑，实测 8 点前发送的触达投诉率是 9 点后的 4.7 倍。9 点是「已起床但未到院高峰」的窗口，同时避开 9:00 门诊高峰的呼叫中心压力（召回扫描错峰到 9:20）。

| 编号 | 规则 | 错误码 |
|---|---|---|
| R-09-4-37 | 已处置的预警不可重复处置 | `"1009030"` |
| R-09-4-38 | `HIGH`/`CRITICAL` 处置意见 ≥10 字 | `"1009031"` |

| R-09-4-41 | **`alert_type = 'RATE_ABNORMAL'` 只允许 `OBSERVE`** | `"1009032"` |
